# PTE Advanced Vocabulary

**Mastered 0/2706**

This collection includes all words that appear twice or less in the PTE test bank. These are the most difficult words in the PTE exam and are more academically specialized. Note: All words in the PTE vocabulary book are from the PTE test bank. We use frequency counting to select words suitable for candidates at different learning stages.
